We instantiate StaMina-4B and StaMina-8B usin<sub>g</sub> the corres<sub>p</sub>ondin<sub>g</sub> Qwen3-VL (Bai et al., 2025a) instruction models. O<sub>ur con</sub>t<sub>r</sub>ib<sub>u</sub>ti<sub>ons are:</sub>

∙ State-conditioned causal counting. A recurrent visual context and typed transition heads distinguish identity bi<sub>r</sub>th<sub>, v</sub>i<sub>s</sub>ibilit<sub>y c</sub>h<sub>ange, an</sub>d <sub>even</sub>t <sub>comp</sub>l<sub>e</sub>ti<sub>on, w</sub>ith <sub>numer</sub>i<sub>ca</sub>l <sub>answers rea</sub>d f<sub>rom</sub> th<sub>e accep</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d <sub>s</sub>t<sub>a</sub>t<sub>e.</sub>

∙ Trajectory-constrained transition learning. ∙ Trajectory data and evaluation. ## 2 Related Work

Streaming visual context. Lon -video benchmarks test tem oral evidence, retrieval, and s atial reasonin (Fu <sub>e</sub>t <sub>a</sub>l<sub>.,</sub> 2025<sub>;</sub> W<sub>u e</sub>t <sub>a</sub>l<sub>.,</sub> 2024<sub>;</sub> Li<sub>u e</sub>t <sub>a</sub>l<sub>.,</sub> 2026<sub>c;</sub> Ni<sub>u e</sub>t <sub>a</sub>l<sub>.,</sub> 2025<sub>;</sub> Li <sub>e</sub>t <sub>a</sub>l<sub>.,</sub> 2026<sub>;</sub> Li<sub>u e</sub>t <sub>a</sub>l<sub>.,</sub> 2026b<sub>;</sub> K<sub>wan e</sub>t <sub>a</sub>l<sub>.,</sub> 2026<sub>;</sub> F<sub>an</sub> et al., 2026). Online evaluation also ties answers to the observations available at a decision time (Lin et al., 2024; Niu et al., 2025). Streamin<sub>g</sub> architectures maintain context throu<sub>g</sub>h memor<sub>y</sub> consolidation, decou<sub>p</sub>led <sub>p</sub>erce<sub>p</sub>tion, or selective retention (Qian et al., 2024, 2025; Xu et al., 2026; Zhan<sub>g</sub> et al., 2025; Yan<sub>g</sub> et al., 2025d; Zen<sub>g</sub> et al., 2025). We <sub>s</sub>t<sub>u</sub>d<sub>y</sub> th<sub>e su</sub>b<sub>sequen</sub>t d<sub>ec</sub>i<sub>s</sub>i<sub>on</sub> th<sub>a</sub>t <sub>conver</sub>t<sub>s</sub> thi<sub>s con</sub>t<sub>ex</sub>t i<sub>n</sub>t<sub>o a pers</sub>i<sub>s</sub>t<sub>en</sub>t <sub>coun</sub>t <sub>up</sub>d<sub>a</sub>t<sub>e.</sub>

Associative memory and state transitions. Test-time training (TTT) adapts parameters online (Sun et al., 2020; Gandelsman et al., 2022; Wan et al., 2025a; Xie et al., 2025; Nath et al., 2026); fast-wei ht la ers use these arameters as recurrent state (Sun et al., 2025; Tandon et al., 2025; Dalal et al., 2025). StreamTTT uses nonlinear fast wei<sub>g</sub>hts with short-window attention (Chen et al., 2026); S<sub>p</sub>atial TTT adds <sub>g</sub>eometr<sub>y</sub>-aware <sub>p</sub>rediction (Liu et al., 2026a); Cambrian-S studies ersistent s atial recall and countin (Yan et al., 2025b). Our continuous branch uses ated linear delta u<sub>p</sub>dates (Yan<sub>g</sub> et al., 2025c; Kimi Team, 2025). The countin<sub>g</sub> mechanism resides in the state-conditioned trans<sup>i</sup>t<sup>i</sup>on mo<sup>d</sup>e<sup>l</sup> an<sup>d i</sup>ts trajectory o<sup>b</sup>ject<sup>i</sup>ve. <sup>A</sup>ct<sup>i</sup>on<sup>S</sup>w<sup>i</sup>tc<sup>h l</sup>earns act<sup>i</sup>on-state c<sup>h</sup>anges w<sup>i</sup>t<sup>h</sup> a conservat<sup>i</sup>ve trans<sup>i</sup>t<sup>i</sup>on loss (Kan<sub>g</sub> et al., 2024). Connectionist tem<sub>p</sub>oral classification (CTC) mar<sub>g</sub>inalizes com<sub>p</sub>atible ali<sub>g</sub>nment <sub>p</sub>aths (Graves et al., 2006), while neural video ali<sub>g</sub>nment learns from weak action annotations (Richard et al., 2018). We <sub>cons</sub>t<sub>ra</sub>i<sub>n opera</sub>t<sub>or-spec</sub>ifi<sub>c pa</sub>th<sub>s</sub> b<sub>y mu</sub>lti<sub>p</sub>l<sub>e coun</sub>t <sub>en</sub>d<sub>po</sub>i<sub>n</sub>t<sub>s, carry p</sub>h<sub>ase pos</sub>t<sub>er</sub>i<sub>ors</sub> b<sub>e</sub>t<sub>ween</sub> th<sub>em, an</sub>d <sub>eva</sub>l<sub>ua</sub>t<sub>e</sub> causa<sup>l</sup> inte<sub>g</sub>er commitments.

Figure 2. StaMina architecture and state-transition learning. (a) Short-window attention and delta memory supply causal features for event transitions and independent visibility/identity updates. DEFER postpones identity association while preserving visible candidates Accepted updates maintain counting state and an evidence log, with separate readouts for general QA and counting. Previous state conditions transition decisions. (b) The event graph defines legal changes. Training sums the probabilities of paths matching supported count endpoints; online inference selects a legal outgoing edge at each observation.

Counting data and embodied state. VSI-Bench, TOMATO, CountLLM, and AV-Reasoner study spatial reasoning, tem<sub>p</sub>oral reasonin<sub>g</sub>, and re<sub>p</sub>etitive or clue-<sub>g</sub>rounded countin<sub>g</sub> (Yan<sub>g</sub> et al., 2025a; Shan<sub>gg</sub>uan et al., 2025; Yao et al., 2025; Lu et al., 2026). SVCBench distin<sub>g</sub>uishes ei<sub>g</sub>ht countin<sub>g</sub> o<sub>p</sub>erators and their trajectories (Liu et al., 2026c). We construct ali<sub>g</sub>ned su<sub>p</sub>ervision from ScanNet, ScanNet++, ARKitScenes, and E<sub>g</sub>o4D (Dai et al., 2017; Yeshwanth et al., 2023; Baruch et al., 2021; Grauman et al., 2022). Count-conditioned <sub>p</sub>redicates connect the resultin<sub>g</sub> state to action eli<sub>g</sub>ibilit<sub>y</sub>, com<sub>p</sub>lementin<sub>g</sub> observation-to-control models (Kim et al., 2024).

## 5 Experiments

## 5.1 Experimental setup

Models and evaluation. We train StaMina-4B and StaMina-8B from Qwen3-VL-4B/8B-Instruct (Bai et al., 2025a). Object endpoints compare the differentiable cumulative count with the accepted identity count under the same rollout. Detailed denominators and supervision strata are given in Appendix G.

StaMina-4B and StaMina-8B initialize from Qwen3-VL-4B/8B-Instruct. Their <sub>p</sub>rocessor, visual tokenization, and <sub>pre</sub>t<sub>ra</sub>i<sub>ne</sub>d <sub>w</sub>idth<sub>s are unc</sub>h<sub>ange</sub>d<sub>.</sub> A d<sub>e</sub>lt<sub>a</sub> b<sub>ranc</sub>h <sub>para</sub>ll<sub>e</sub>l<sub>s a</sub>tt<sub>en</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> i<sub>n every</sub> 4th d<sub>eco</sub>d<sub>er</sub> bl<sub>oc</sub>k<sub>,</sub> b<sub>e</sub>f<sub>ore</sub> th<sub>e</sub> f<sub>ee</sub>d<sub>-</sub> <sup>f</sup>orwar<sup>d</sup> su<sup>bl</sup>ayer. <sup>E</sup>ac<sup>h b</sup>ranc<sup>h</sup> concatenates <sup>8 h</sup>ea<sup>d</sup>s o<sup>f</sup> w<sup>id</sup>t<sup>h 64</sup>, projects t<sup>h</sup>e<sup>i</sup>r rea<sup>d</sup>outs to t<sup>h</sup>e <sup>d</sup>eco<sup>d</sup>er w<sup>id</sup>t<sup>h</sup>, an<sup>d</sup> <sub>a</sub>dd<sub>s a s</sub>i<sub>gmo</sub>id<sub>-ga</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d <sub>res</sub>id<sub>ua</sub>l t<sub>o</sub> th<sub>e a</sub>tt<sub>en</sub>ti<sub>on ou</sub>t<sub>pu</sub>t<sub>.</sub> E<sub>ac</sub>h <sub>square</sub> $A _ { 0 }$ i<sub>s a s</sub>h<sub>are</sub>d t<sub>ra</sub>i<sub>na</sub>bl<sub>e parame</sub>t<sub>er</sub> i<sub>n</sub>iti<sub>a</sub>li<sub>ze</sub>d t<sub>o</sub> zero an<sup>d</sup> cop<sup>i</sup>e<sup>d</sup> at a new trajectory. <sup>R</sup>etent<sup>i</sup>on an<sup>d</sup> wr<sup>i</sup>te gates are s<sup>i</sup>gmo<sup>id</sup>s o<sup>f</sup> separate <sup>li</sup>near project<sup>i</sup>ons o<sup>f</sup> t<sup>h</sup>e to<sup>k</sup>en in<sub>p</sub>ut, scalar <sub>p</sub>er head. The channel-wise retention com<sub>p</sub>arison chan<sub>g</sub>es this <sub>g</sub>ate alone (Kimi Team, 2025). Slow wei<sub>g</sub>hts are fixed at de<sub>p</sub>lo<sub>y</sub>ment. Scorin<sub>g</sub> heads use the Gaussian error linear unit (GELU) activation (Hendr<sub>y</sub>cks and Gim<sub>p</sub>el, 2016).

Object losses and gradient boundaries. Soft visible and birth totals are

$$
\widetilde { N } _ { k } = \sum _ { i } \nu _ { k i } , \quad \widetilde { b } _ { k } = \sum _ { i } \nu _ { k i } p _ { k i } ( \mathrm { N E W } ) , \quad \widetilde { U } _ { k } = \sum _ { j \leq k } \widetilde { b } _ { j } .\tag{12}
$$
